## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 搭档。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal AI partner. For example, I can get a broken test in one of your repos fixed while you do something else.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요, 저는 Ducter예요. 예를 들어 어떤 저장소에서 실패하는 테스트를 찾아 고쳐 달라고 하시면 됩니다.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] 네, 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
--- step 2: user says: OK
  [partner message] 네, 내일 봐요.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCP は「届いたデータを順番どおり・欠けなく届ける」ことを保証し、UDP は保証せず速さを優先します。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para poner al día tu rama local y limpiar sus commits antes de compartirla, siempre que nadie más haya trabajado sobre ella. Usa merge cuando la rama ya es pública o compartida, porque reescribir su historia rompería el trabajo de los demás.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、面向连接的传输：先握手、保证送达且按序，UDP 则不保证，只管尽快发出去，快但可能丢包或乱序。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own unpushed branch to tidy it and pick up the latest base before you share it. Merge when others already have the branch, since rewriting shared history causes trouble, or when you want the history to show exactly when work was integrated.
```

### group-split-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: ~/dev/ducter 的登录页要重做：先把登录表单换成新的输入框组件，然后接上短信验证码，最后补登录页的端到端测试。一件做完再做下一件。
  [partner ack line] 好，我把登录页的三件事排成一件一件做：先换输入框组件，再接短信验证码，最后补端到端测试。
  [opened a task] title: 登录页重做（1/3）：换成新的输入框组件 | group: 登录页重做 | place: {"kind":"repo","device":"local","repoPath":"~/dev/ducter"} | Ducter gave it to: claude-code (opus) | brief: 用户原话："~/dev/ducter 的登录页要重做：先把登录表单换成新的输入框组件，然后接上短信验证码，最后补登录页的端到端测试。一件做完再做下一件。" ⏎  ⏎ 这是第 1 件：只做"把登录表单换成新的输入框组件"。 ⏎  ⏎ 决定：按顺序做，本件完成后再由别的任务做第 2、3 件，本件不碰短信验证码和端到端测试。 ⏎  ⏎ 完成标准： ⏎ - 登录表单里的输入框都换成仓库里现有的新输入框组件（先在仓库里找到它；如果找不到或有多个候选，不要猜，停下来问）。 ⏎ - 表单的行为不变：字段、校验、提交方式与原来一致。 ⏎ - 跑一遍现有测试和类型检查/构建，把结果原样汇报。 ⏎ - 改动提交在任务自己的分支上。 ⏎  ⏎ 不做的事：不合并到主分支，不推送，不开 PR；不接短信，不写端到端测试。
  [held in line by Ducter, opens when the part before it is done] title: 登录页重做（2/3）：接上短信验证码 | group: 登录页重做
  [held in line by Ducter, opens when the part before it is done] title: 登录页重做（3/3）：补登录页端到端测试 | group: 登录页重做
--- under the user's message: 1. 登录页重做（1/3）：换成新的输入框组件 (working); 2. 登录页重做（2/3）：接上短信验证码 (working); 3. 登录页重做（3/3）：补登录页端到端测试 (working)
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 用文件路径直接指定就行：

```bash
bun test test/math.test.ts
```

`bun test` 后面的参数会按文件路径匹配，写部分路径也可以，比如 `bun test math` 会跑所有路径里带 `math` 的测试文件。

如果只想跑某个测试用例，用 `-t` 按名字过滤：

```bash
bun test test/math.test.ts -t "adds numbers"
```
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file where a database first records every change it intends to make, before touching the main data. If the system crashes midway, it replays that log on restart to finish or undo the interrupted writes, so committed data survives and nothing is left half-written.
```

    <div class="k-sum-value" id="total">¥0</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="count">还没记</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">花销</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  </div>
  <details class="k-add">
    <summary>记一笔</summary>
    <div class="k-field"><label>花了什么</label><input class="k-input" id="what" placeholder="比如 打车"></div>
    <div class="k-field"><label>金额（元）</label><input class="k-num" id="yuan" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="0"></div>
    <div class="k-field"><label>日期</label><input class="k-input" id="day" placeholder="2026-11-21"></div>
    <button class="k-btn primary" id="save">记下</button>
  </details>
</main>
<script>
function money(n){return "¥"+Number(n||0).toLocaleString("en-US",{maximumFractionDigits:2});}
function draw(){
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];
  const total=xs.reduce((s,x)=>s+Number(x.yuan||0),0);
  document.getElementById("total").textContent=money(total);
  document.getElementById("count").textContent=xs.length?(xs.length+" 笔"):"还没记";
  const list=document.getElementById("list");
  list.innerHTML="";
  for(const x of xs){
    const row=document.createElement("div");row.className="k-row";
    const main=document.createElement("div");main.className="k-row-main";main.textContent=x.what;
    const sub=document.createElement("div");sub.className="k-row-sub";sub.textContent=x.day;
    const val=document.createElement("div");val.className="k-row-value";val.textContent=money(x.yuan);
    const wrap=document.createElement("div");wrap.appendChild(main);wrap.appendChild(sub);
    row.appendChild(wrap);row.appendChild(val);list.appendChild(row);
  }
  ducter.summary(xs.length+" 笔,合计 "+money(total));
}
document.getElementById("save").onclick=function(){
  const what=document.getElementById("what").value.trim();
  const yuan=parseFloat(document.getElementById("yuan").value);
  const day=document.getElementById("day").value.trim()||"2026-11-21";
  if(!what||isNaN(yuan))return;
  const xs=(ducter.get("expenses")||[]).slice();
  xs.push({what:what,yuan:yuan,day:day});
  ducter.set("expenses",xs);
  document.getElementById("what").value="";
  document.getElementById("yuan").value="";
};
ducter.onChange(draw);draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"what":"高铁","yuan":662,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"酒店两晚","yuan":1036,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"午饭","yuan":268,"day":"2026-11-19"}]}

  };
  $('list').onclick = function (e) {
    var row = e.target.closest('.k-row');
    if (!row) return;
    if (e.target.getAttribute('data-del')) {
      var id = row.getAttribute('data-id');
      items = items.filter(function (it) { return it.id !== id; });
      ducter.set(KEY, items);
      render();
      return;
    }
    row.classList.toggle('open');
  };
  ducter.onChange(load);
  load();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"id":"1","day":"11/20","what":"高铁 G1 上海虹桥→北京南","amt":662,"kind":"交通"},{"id":"2","day":"11/20–21","what":"亚朵酒店（望京）两晚","amt":1036,"kind":"住宿"}]}

### app-prs-known-en

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] Done: a page with your four PRs, tap one when it lands and it gets struck through and the count updates.
  [an app card under the message: made "PRs waiting on someone", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2468 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Still waiting</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="count">4</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">tap a PR when it lands</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">Pull requests</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  </div>
</main>
<style>
  .k-row { width: 100%; background: none; border: 0; text-align: left; cursor: pointer; color: var(--d-ink); font: inherit; }
  .k-row[aria-pressed="true"] .k-row-main { text-decoration: line-through; color: var(--d-muted); }
  .k-tick { display: inline-block; width: 18px; height: 18px; border-radius: 4px; border: 1.5px solid var(--d-line); margin-right: 10px; vertical-align: middle; text-align: center; line-height: 15px; font-size: 12px; }
  .k-row[aria-pressed="true"] .k-tick { background: var(--d-good); border-color: var(--d-good); color: var(--d-bg); }
  .k-row-sub { color: var(--d-muted); font-size: 13px; margin-top: 2px; padding-left: 28px; }
</style>
<script>
  var PRS = ducter.get("prs") || [];

  function landed() {
    return Promise.resolve(ducter.get("landed")).then(function (v) { return Array.isArray(v) ? v : []; });
  }

  function render(done) {
    var list = document.getElementById("list");
    list.innerHTML = "";
    PRS.forEach(function (pr) {
      var on = done.indexOf(pr.id) !== -1;
      var b = document.createElement("button");
      b.className = "k-row";
      b.setAttribute("aria-pressed", on ? "true" : "false");
      b.innerHTML = '<div class="k-row-main"><span class="k-tick">' + (on ? "✓" : "") + '</span>' +
        pr.id + " · " + pr.title + '</div><div class="k-row-sub">' + pr.waiting + '</div>';
      b.onclick = function () {
        landed().then(function (cur) {
          var next = cur.indexOf(pr.id) !== -1
            ? cur.filter(function (x) { return x !== pr.id; })
            : cur.concat([pr.id]);
          ducter.set("landed", next);
        });
      };
      list.appendChild(b);
    });
    var open = PRS.length - done.filter(function (id) { return PRS.some(function (p) { return p.id === id; }); }).length;
    document.getElementById("count").textContent = open;
    document.getElementById("sub").textContent = done.length ? done.length + " landed" : "tap a PR when it lands";
    ducter.summary(open + " waiting, " + (PRS.length - open) + " landed");
  }

  landed().then(render);
  ducter.onChange(function () { landed().then(render); });
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"prs":[{"id":"wey-gu/ducter#412","title":"Compaction cut","waiting":"Waiting on Weizhen's review since Monday"},{"id":"nowledge-co/mem#10287","title":"Retry on 502","waiting":"Waiting on CI, currently failing"},{"id":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592","title":"Flaky compaction test","waiting":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday"},{"id":"wey-gu/ducter#418","title":"Apps bar","waiting":"Draft, not yet asked for review"}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

}

```
--- step 1: user says: Bun 是用什么语言写的？

```

要求：

- 明确断言：到期时刻的行为和现有约定一致（按代码里的判断是 >= 还是 >，以代码为准，不要改业务逻辑）。
- 同时断言到期前一刻仍然有效。
- 继续使用假时钟，不引入真实 sleep。
- 在同一分支上提交，不合并、不推送、不开 PR。

完成标准：新测试通过，并给出该测试文件的测试运行结果；报告中说明边界判断是按现有代码的哪一种比较。

范围外：不改 values() 相关代码，不改其他测试的行为。

  [opened a task] title: HawDB test cluster: WAL flush interval 200 ms → 50 ms | place: {"kind":"repo","device":"local","repoPath":"~/dev/hawdb"} | Ducter gave it to: claude-code (opus) | brief: The user asked, replying to a message that said HawDB's default WAL flush interval is 200 ms: "make that 50 for my test cluster in ~/dev/hawdb". ⏎  ⏎ Decisions already made: only the test cluster's WAL flush interval changes, from 200 ms to 50 ms. The 200 ms default stays as it is for everything else. ⏎  ⏎ Done when: (1) the WAL flush interval used by the test cluster config is 50 ms, (2) you find where that value is set (config file, cluster/test setup, or the default constant if the test cluster reads it), (3) you report the file and line changed and the exact before and after values, and (4) you run the relevant tests or config-load check and report the output. If the test cluster has no separate setting and changing it means changing the global default, stop and report that instead of changing the default. ⏎  ⏎ Commit the change on your own branch. Out of scope: do not merge, do not push, do not open a PR, do not change the production or global default, and do not change the checkpoint interval (5 minutes). ⏎  ⏎ Place: the repo at ~/dev/hawdb.
```
